1.  **Analyze the Request:**
    *   **Task:** Extract a compliance matrix from the provided RFP text.
    *   **Input:** RFP text for Solicitation ID: 3202600177 (Texas Workforce Commission - Purchasing from People with Disabilities).
    *   **Output Format:** JSON array of requirements objects.
    *   **Rules:**
        *   `verbatim_text`: Exact sentence(s) from RFP. No paraphrasing.
        *   `section_ref`: RFP's own numbering (e.g., "2.1", "Section IV.B"). Use nearest heading if unnumbered.
        *   `type`: "shall" (binding), "should" (advisory), "info" (administrative facts).
        *   `category`: eligibility, deliverable, deadline, form, evaluation. Deadlines with dates -> "deadline" + ISO `due_date`.
        *   `page`: PDF page number (1-indexed).
        *   `id`: Sequential (R-001, R-002, ...).
        *   **Constraint:** Do NOT invent requirements. If no requirements, return empty array.
        *   **Constraint:** Split sentences with multiple obligations into two rows.
        *   **Constraint:** Cover the final page, including attachments/exhibits (though none are explicitly detailed in the text provided beyond the Table of Contents and Definitions).
        *   **Constraint:** The provided text is long (pages 1-23 shown, likely more in a real scenario, but I must work with what's provided). I need to scan the provided text for specific requirements.

The user wants me to extract a compliance matrix from the provided RFP text.
I need to follow the specific rules provided in the system prompt:
1.  **verbatim_text**: Exact sentence(s) from the RFP. No paraphrasing.
2.  **One requirement per row**: Split sentences with multiple obligations.
3.  **section_ref**: RFP's own numbering (e.g., "2.1", "3.2.1"). Use nearest heading if unnumbered.
4.  **type**: "shall" (binding), "should" (advisory), "info" (administrative facts).
5.  **category**: eligibility, deliverable, deadline, form, evaluation. Deadlines with dates get "deadline" and ISO due_date.
6.  **page**: PDF page number (1-indexed).
7.  **id**: Sequential (R-001, R-002, ...).
8.  **Do NOT invent requirements**: Only extract what is explicitly stated.

I will scan the text page by page and identify requirements.

**Page 24:**
- "The Successful Respondent shall assist the Successor Respondent by providing information and input as necessary to ensure smooth transition wherein services are not disrupted." -> Binding (shall), Deliverable/Service.
- "Additionally, to the extent the Successful Respondent has any contracts for Non-Uniformly Priced Services that have been approved under the PPD Program, the Successful Respondent shall include in such contracts a provision permitting assignment to a successor CNA and shall ensure all such assignments occur (with notice to TWC) during the transition period." -> Binding (shall), Deliverable/Service.
- "The Successful Respondent must track Texas legislation and keep abreast of changing factors that could impact the State Use Program and inform TWC of such factors." -> Binding (must), Deliverable/Service.
- "The Successful Respondent must attend Purchasing from People with Disabilities’ Advisory Committee meetings and provide support for the Committee as requested by TWC." -> Binding (must), Deliverable/Service.
- "The Successful Respondent must perform other duties as assigned by TWC in support of this Contract." -> Binding (must), Deliverable/Service.

**Page 25:**

    - e. "Respondent has submitted the Response in accordance with Article IV Response Submission Instructions; see also Section 4.6 Required Forms and Submission Documents;" -> Binding ("has submitted"), Deliverable.
    - f. "Respondent who receives an overall score on the VPTS of ”D” or below shall be disqualified from consideration for award." -> Binding ("shall"), Deliverable.
- 5.3 INITIAL COMPLIANCE SCREENING
    - "TWC will review Responses for initial compliance with the Submission requirements listed in Article IV and for demonstrated ability to meet Section 5.2 Minimum Qualifications (Pass/Fail) required to advance to evaluations." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "Failure to meet the minimum qualifications will result in the disqualification of the Response." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "TWC will automatically disqualify any Response that does not include one or more of the completed and signed (as applicable) documents." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "All Responses will be screened for inclusion of all required information prior to release to the evaluation team." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "TWC may exclude from further consideration for selection any non-responsive Response or portion of a Response." -> Binding ("may"), Deliverable.
- 5.4 RESPONSE EVALUATION
    - "Each member of the evaluation committee will read the Responses in preparation for evaluation." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "The evaluation committee will score all Responses that pass initial screening in Section 5.3 Initial Compliance Screening, even if there is only one viable Response." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "Responses will be scored against the criteria in Section 5.5 Evaluation Criteria." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "Responses will be evaluated utilizing aggregated individual scoring outlined in this Article V Evaluation and Award." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "The individual evaluators’ scores will be aggregated and weighted, resulting in the final Response Score, unless TWC requests a Revised Response from those Respondents in the Competitive Range." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
- 5.5 EVALUATION CRITERIA
    - "TWC shall consistently evaluate and score Responses in accordance with the following criteria." -> Binding ("shall"), Deliverable.
    - a. "Ability to Perform Services (50%)" -> Info.
    - 1. "Section 4.7.4 Response to Requirements;" -> Info.
    - b. "Past Performance, Experience, Qualifications, and References (40%)" -> Info.
    - 1. "Section 4.7.3.2 Experience/Respondent Organization Narrative;" -> Info.
    - 2. "Form 3 References (including Cancelled Contract References) (Section 4.8.1 Similar Contracts, and Section 4.8.2 Contract Performance)." -> Info.
    - c. "Price or Reasonableness of Price Proposal (10%)" -> Info.
- 5.6 COMPETITIVE RANGE
    - "The Competitive Range will consist of the Responses that receive the highest scores, or most satisfactory ratings based on the published evaluation criteria and procedures governing this procurement." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "Cutoff for the Competitive Range will be based on the “natural break” in scores and on reasoned judgment that Responses below the cutoff cannot be made successful through clarification and negotiation." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "TWC is not obligated to enforce a natural break in scores and reserves the right to advance as many or as few Responses as qualified under these criteria to achieve the best value for the state of Texas." -> Binding ("reserves"), Deliverable.
- 5.7 DISCUSSIONS AND REVISED RESPONSES
    - "After the written Response evaluation, Respondent may be selected for invitation to additional evaluation activities that may include one or more of the following: oral presentations, clarifications, discussions, Proof of Concept, demonstrations, and site visits." -> Binding ("may"), Deliverable.
    - "Advancement will be determined by the Competitive Range." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "A Respondent that does not meet the advancement criteria in this Section will not be invited to participate in additional evaluation activities." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "The Final Written Response Score will be considered the Total Score, and the Respondent may not be considered further for Contract award." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "To further identify the Respondent providing the best value, Respondents selected for additional evaluation activities will be the final group of Respondents eligible for award." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "Additional evaluation activities will allow for revised points for the Respondent’s Final Written Response Score to replace the original technical score." -> Binding ("will"), Deliverable.
    - "This will be the Final Written Response Score with any adjusted points received, if applicable." -> Info.
    - "TWC may require additional evaluation activities from any or all Respondents to make a determination for award recommendation." -> Binding ("may"), Deliverable.
    - "Information from the" -> (Text cuts off, ignore).

I need to be careful with the "type" classification.
- "shall", "must", "will", "required to" -> "shall"
- "should", "is encouraged to" -> "should"
- Administrative facts (deadlines, page limits, etc.) -> "info" (unless they have a modal verb like "must" or "shall").
- However, the prompt says: "type: 'shall' for binding (shall/must/will/required), 'should' for advisory, 'info' for administrative facts with no modal verb."
- So if a sentence says "The Organization Narrative must not exceed two (2) pages." -> "must" -> "shall".
- If a sentence says "Respondents should have five (5) years of experience" -> "should" -> "should".
- If a sentence says "Late submissions will not be accepted." -> "will" -> "shall".
- If a sentence says "TWC will not reimburse Respondents..." -> "will" -> "shall".

I need to ensure I don't miss the "deadline" category for dates.
- "no later than 2:00 PM CT on March 5, 2026" -> deadline.
- "within the last five (5) years" -> info (timeframe for reference).
- "within thirty (30) Business Days" -> deadline.
- "annually throughout the term of the Contract" -> info (frequency).
- "one hundred eighty (180) calendar days following the solicitation closing date" -> info (duration).

=== CHUNK BREAK ===

The user wants me to extract a compliance matrix from the provided RFP text.
I need to identify requirements that meet specific criteria:
- Binding language: shall, must, will (vendor obligation), is required to, may not.
- Advisory language directed at the vendor: should, is encouraged to.
- Administrative facts a bidder must act on: submission deadlines, question deadlines, conference dates, required forms, page limits, copy counts, delivery addresses, eligibility/certification prerequisites, evaluation criteria and weights.

Rules:
1. verbatim_text: EXACT sentence(s) from the RFP. No paraphrasing.
2. One requirement per row. Split if two independent obligations.
3. section_ref: RFP's own numbering.
4. type: "shall" for binding, "should" for advisory, "info" for administrative facts with no modal verb.
5. category: eligibility | deliverable | deadline | form | evaluation.
6. page: PDF page number (1-indexed).
7. id: Sequential (R-001, R-002...).
8. Do NOT invent requirements.

I need to be careful with "type".
- "shall", "must", "will" (vendor obligation) -> "shall"
- "should", "is encouraged to" -> "should"
- Administrative facts (deadlines, forms, etc.) without modal verb -> "info"
- However, if it's a deadline, it's "deadline" category. If it's a form requirement, it's "form" category.
- The prompt says: "type: 'shall' for binding (shall/must/will/required), 'should' for advisory, 'info' for administrative facts with no modal verb."
- So if a sentence has "must", type is "shall". If it has "should", type is "should". If it has no modal verb but is a fact (e.g. "The contract expires..."), type is "info".
- Wait, "deadline" category usually implies a date. If the sentence is "Respondents shall submit... no later than 2:00 PM CT on March 5, 2026", type is "shall", category "deadline".
- If the sentence is "The contract expires two (2) years after execution date", type is "info", category "deadline" (maybe? or just info). The prompt says "deadlines with dates always get category 'deadline'". So I should include it.
- "The Response must include a certification..." -> Binding, Form.
- "The Successful Respondent must make payment within thirty (30) days of the TWC’s demand." -> Binding, Deadline.
- "The Successful Respondent shall acquire insurance..." -> Binding, Deliverable.

I need to check if "will" counts as binding. The prompt says "Binding language: shall, must, will (vendor obligation)". So "will" is binding.
I need to check if "constitutes failure" is binding. Yes, it's a consequence of failure to comply, implying the obligation exists.
I need to check if "agrees to" is binding. Yes.
I need to check if "must" is binding. Yes.
I need to check if "shall" is binding. Yes.

I need to check if "should" is advisory. Yes.

I need to check if "info" is for administrative facts with no modal verb.
Example: "The Contract Term will commence on the fully executed Contract date and expire two (2) years after execution date." -> "will" is a modal verb. Is it vendor obligation? No, it's TWC's action. So type "info".
Example: "The Successful Respondent must make payment within thirty (30) days of the TWC’s demand." -> Vendor obligation. Type "shall".

I need to ensure I don't include sentences that are just informational about TWC's rights or actions unless they impose a duty on the vendor.
Example: "TWC will withhold Remedies from payments..." -> TWC action. Info.
Example: "TWC may negotiate a Contract..." -> TWC action. Info.

I need to ensure I don't include sentences that are just informational about the contract term unless they impose a duty.
Example: "The Contract Term will commence on the fully executed Contract date and expire two (2) years after execution date." -> Info.
Example: "The Contract is contingent upon the continued availability of funding." -> Info.

I need to ensure I don't include sentences that are just informational about the evaluation process unless they impose a duty.

I need to ensure I don't include sentences that are just informational about the waiver unless they impose a duty.
Example: "SUBMISSION OF ANY DOCUMENT TO TWC IN RESPONSE TO THIS SOLICITATION CONSTITUTES AN IRREVOCABLE WAIVER..." -> This is a statement of fact about the submission, which imposes a duty (you must indemnify). So binding.
